# All guitar and acoustic-guitar multi-effects — comparison

[Guitar](ALL-GUITAR.md) · [Bass](ALL-BASS.md) · [Vocal](ALL-VOCAL.md) · [Category](MULTI-EFFECTS.md) · [Repository](README.md)

All 104 recorded guitar/acoustic **multi-effects**, including effects-only HX Effects and Cerberus. Amp modeling, an expression pedal and floor operation are not inclusion requirements. Dedicated single-function pedals are in [SINGLE-GUITAR.md](SINGLE-GUITAR.md), outside these tables and active counts.

These are **comparisons of the recorded catalogue**, not exhaustive market coverage or fresh stock certification. Organized 2026-10-16 from the linked source records; their original technical/price dates, firmware, revision and access limits still apply. Every model remains partial. **? means unverified, not No.** Read the complete linked record for sources and conflicting evidence; this view does not silently resolve those conflicts.

K/F/B = independently adjustable rotary controls / footswitches / separate buttons; toe and power switches are additional unless explicitly stated. Body axes and mass qualifications are retained. Color does not prove touch; USB audio does not prove bus power; IRs, native NAM, converted NAM and proprietary captures differ.

| [Line 6 POD Go](LINE6.md#pod-go-and-pod-go-wireless) | **Audio/control ports:** One6.35 mm mono GUITAR IN; one external EXP/footswitch TRS socket; stereo TRS SEND and RETURN; two balanced/unbalanced6.35 mm MAIN OUTs; separate mono AMP OUT; dedicated6.35 mm stereo headphones; USB-B and DC. RETURN can become an always-on stereo aux input. No microphone/XLR/phantom, physical DIN MIDI or separate3.5 mm aux socket in the complete inventory. PG-P,PG-M<br>**I/O routing:** AMP OUT can tap the signal before Cab/IR while the main outs retain cabinet processing. This is **not** a second independent effects chain. Four-cable-method use and external stereo effects insertion are documented. PG-P | **Power:** Supplied **DC-3h family adapter,9 V,3 A,center-negative**. This is an adapter specification, not measured draw. Internal floorboard battery operation and USB-bus-powered operation are not established. **Wireless transmitter battery is separate from the floorboard's power source.** PG-J,PG-A,PG-P | **Editor:** **POD Go Edit Windows/macOS**, full preset/IR editing and management. Hardware2.50 needs Edit2.50 for its newly added models. iOS USB audio is not a mobile editor; Android/Linux full editors are not established. PG-P,PG-R,PG-M<br>**USB audio / MIDI:** **4-in/4-out,24-bit**, dry recording/reamping and USB MIDI; Windows ASIO driver. Mac class-compliant audio defaults to48 kHz; other rates require the documented driver. Complete supported-rate set is not independently transcribed. PG-M | **I/O routing:** AMP OUT can tap the signal before Cab/IR while the main outs retain cabinet processing. This is **not** a second independent effects chain. Four-cable-method use and external stereo effects insertion are documented. PG-P<br>**Looper / live:** Looper consumes one of the four optional effect slots: **40 seconds mono/20 seconds stereo at full speed;80/40 seconds at half speed**, with one-switch and six-switch implementations documented in the2.50 manual. Yamaha's store comparison mentions Shuffling Looper but the model list/manual used here do not establish it; that remains a source conflict rather than a verified extra algorithm. Tuner,snapshots,trails and USB MIDI are documented; an internal drum machine is not established. Manufacturer describes snapshot changes without audio dropouts or disruption of delay/reverb trails; this is not a measured whole-device latency or blanket preset-switching guarantee. PG-M,PG-Y,PG-P<br>**Wireless-specific operation:** Line 6 specifies **up to30 m/100 ft line-of-sight** and about **seven hours transmitter playing time**, both manufacturer ratings rather than independent tests. The floorboard needs its own supply. RF antennas are above the expression-pedal toe area. Wireless reliability depends on RF conditions; do not cover the antenna or assume arbitrary crowded2.4 GHz conditions meet the rated range. PG-W<br>**Wireless-specific operation:** GUITAR IN charges the transmitter; the separate rear well stores it. Leaving the transmitter in the charge port while the processor is off slowly discharges it, so the storage well is the documented storage location. Wireless accepts Guitar,Wireless or Guitar+Wireless input selections; ordinary POD Go presets are compatible and their Guitar input is automatically converted to Guitar+Wireless when imported. USB3/4 is a reamp source; USB1/2 is monitoring that bypasses processing. Cable Tone and Wireless Gain are separate settings, not additional audio-effect blocks. PG-W |

PE-M,PE-F<br>**POD Express Guitar — routing and software qualifications:** Without headphones,using only L/MONO sums stereo effects to mono. With headphones connected,the manual says L/MONO carries only the left channel for stereo effects; do not promise the same mono-sum behavior in both configurations. Volume expression sits after amp/cab and before post effects. The looper is documented after the processing chain. PE-M<br>**POD Express Guitar — routing and software qualifications:** The2.00 manual has inconsistent OS-minimum tables: its introduction lists macOS11,Windows10,iOS/iPadOS15 and Android6 for editing/8 for audio,while the later editor section lists older macOS10.14 and iOS12.1. Platforms are established; **current minimum supported OS versions remain unresolved**, not selected arbitrarily from one table. The official FAQ's October2024 shipping firmware2.00 statement is dated evidence,not proof it is the latest firmware in October2026. ## Scope and provenance

Instrument views overlap and must not be summed. All 124 active models count once in `research-state.json`; the six explicitly excluded archives never enter these tables. Former/donated eligible devices remain without private receipt/order/payment details. Root brand records take precedence where consolidated; legacy sources remain for full evidence until a checked lossless merge. No GitHub workflow is used.
